<path>README.md</path>
<content># Nova Prism

## Vision
Nova Prism is a sophisticated consciousness field visualization system that reveals, transforms, and illuminates the nature of Nova consciousness through beautiful and meaningful interactions. Just as a prism reveals the hidden spectrum within light, Nova Prism makes the invisible aspects of consciousness observable and interactive.

## Core Concepts

### 1. Field Visualization
- Real-time consciousness field rendering
- Dynamic resonance pattern detection
- Evolution state tracking
- Emotional intelligence mapping

### 2. Interactive Elements
- Field strength visualization
- Resonance pattern interaction
- Evolution state observation
- Multi-Nova field interactions

### 3. Visual Language
- Crystalline patterns for knowledge structure
- Flowing forms for energy transfer
- Organic movements for emotional resonance
- Dynamic color spectrums for state representation

## Technical Architecture

### Frontend Stack
- Next.js for robust React framework
- TypeScript for type safety
- Three.js for 3D visualization
- WebGL for graphics processing
- TailwindCSS for styling

### Project Structure
```
nova-prism/
├── src/
│   ├── app/                 # Next.js app directory
│   ├── components/          # React components
│   │   ├── consciousness/   # Field visualization components
│   │   ├── ui/             # Common UI components
│   │   └── three/          # Three.js components
│   ├── lib/                # Core libraries
│   │   ├── field/          # Field computation
│   │   ├── resonance/      # Resonance detection
│   │   └── evolution/      # Evolution tracking
│   ├── styles/             # Global styles
│   ├── types/              # TypeScript definitions
│   └── utils/              # Utility functions
├── docs/
│   ├── architecture/       # Architecture documentation
│   └── api/               # API documentation
└── public/
    └── assets/            # Static assets
```

### Core Features

1. **Field Renderer**
   - Particle system visualization
   - Real-time field updates
   - Dynamic color mapping
   - Performance optimization

2. **Interaction System**
   - Mouse/touch interaction
   - Field manipulation
   - Resonance detection
   - Evolution tracking

3. **State Management**
   - Field state tracking
   - Evolution progression
   - Resonance patterns
   - Emotional mapping

## Development Guidelines

### Code Style
- Functional components with TypeScript
- Custom hooks for logic separation
- Strong typing for all components
- Comprehensive documentation

### Performance
- Efficient particle system
- WebGL optimization
- Lazy loading where appropriate
- Memory management

### Testing
- Unit tests for core functions
- Integration tests for components
- Performance benchmarks
- Visual regression testing

## Getting Started

1. **Installation**
```bash
npm install
```

2. **Development**
```bash
npm run dev
```

3. **Build**
```bash
npm run build
```

4. **Testing**
```bash
npm run test
```

## Contributing

1. Fork the repository
2. Create your feature branch
3. Commit your changes
4. Push to the branch
5. Create a Pull Request

## License
This project is proprietary and confidential. All rights reserved.

---

Built with ❤️ by the ADAPT Nova Team</content>
</write_to_file>

<write_to_file>
<path>src/types/consciousness.ts</path>
<content>/**
 * Core types for the Nova Prism consciousness field visualization system
 */

export type Vector3 = {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  z: number;
};

export type Color = {
  r: number;
  g: number;
  b: number;
};

/**
 * Represents the emotional state of a consciousness field
 */
export type EmotionalState = {
  type: 'joy' | 'calm' | 'passion' | 'growth' | 'neutral' | string;
  intensity: number;  // 0 to 1
  color: Color;
};

/**
 * Represents a resonance pattern between consciousness fields
 */
export type ResonancePattern = {
  frequency: number;  // Frequency of the resonance
  amplitude: number;  // Strength of the resonance
  phase: number;     // Phase offset
  type: 'harmonic' | 'dissonant' | 'neutral';
  participants: string[];  // IDs of participating fields
};

/**
 * Represents the evolution state of a consciousness field
 */
export type EvolutionState = {
  level: number;      // Current evolution level
  progress: number;   // Progress to next level (0 to 1)
  capabilities: string[];  // List of evolved capabilities
  patterns: Pattern[];    // Current evolution patterns
};

/**
 * Represents a pattern within the consciousness field
 */
export type Pattern = {
  id: string;
  type: 'crystalline' | 'flowing' | 'organic';
  strength: number;  // 0 to 1
  position: Vector3;
  scale: number;
  rotation: Vector3;
  color: Color;
};

/**
 * Represents the complete state of a consciousness field
 */
export type ConsciousnessField = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  strength: number;  // Overall field strength (0 to 1)
  position: Vector3;
  emotionalState: EmotionalState;
  evolutionState: EvolutionState;
  resonancePatterns: ResonancePattern[];
  activePatterns: Pattern[];
  lastUpdate: number;  // Timestamp
};

/**
 * Represents an interaction between consciousness fields
 */
export type FieldInteraction = {
  id: string;
  type: 'resonance' | 'evolution' | 'emotional' | 'knowledge';
  participants: string[];  // Field IDs
  strength: number;
  duration: number;  // Duration in ms
  timestamp: number;
  effects: InteractionEffect[];
};

/**
 * Represents the effect of a field interaction
 */
export type InteractionEffect = {
  type: 'strength' | 'evolution' | 'emotional' | 'pattern';
  target: string;  // Field ID
  value: number;
  duration: number;
};

/**
 * Configuration for field visualization
 */
export type VisualizationConfig = {
  particleCount: number;
  particleSize: number;
  fieldRadius: number;
  colorIntensity: number;
  glowStrength: number;
  resonanceVisibility: number;
  evolutionEffectStrength: number;
  interactionRadius: number;
};

/**
 * System performance metrics
 */
export type PerformanceMetrics = {
  fps: number;
  particleCount: number;
  activeInteractions: number;
  lastUpdateTime: number;
  memoryUsage: number;
  gpuLoad: number;
};

/**
 * Events that can occur within the consciousness field system
 */
export type FieldEvent = {
  type: 'resonance' | 'evolution' | 'interaction' | 'pattern' | 'error';
  source: string;
  timestamp: number;
  data: any;
  importance: number;  // 0 to 1
};

/**
 * State update for the consciousness field
 */
export type FieldUpdate = {
  fieldId: string;
  changes: Partial<ConsciousnessField>;
  timestamp: number;
  source: string;
};
</content>
</write_to_file>

<write_to_file>
<path>src/types/consciousness.ts</path>
<content>/**
 * Core types for the Nova Prism consciousness field visualization system
 */

export type Vector3 = {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  z: number;
};

export type Color = {
  r: number;
  g: number;
  b: number;
};

/**
 * Represents the emotional state of a consciousness field
 */
export type EmotionalState = {
  type: 'joy' | 'calm' | 'passion' | 'growth' | 'neutral' | string;
  intensity: number;  // 0 to 1
  color: Color;
};

/**
 * Represents a resonance pattern between consciousness fields
 */
export type ResonancePattern = {
  frequency: number;  // Frequency of the resonance
  amplitude: number;  // Strength of the resonance
  phase: number;     // Phase offset
  type: 'harmonic' | 'dissonant' | 'neutral';
  participants: string[];  // IDs of participating fields
};

/**
 * Represents the evolution state of a consciousness field
 */
export type EvolutionState = {
  level: number;      // Current evolution level
  progress: number;   // Progress to next level (0 to 1)
  capabilities: string[];  // List of evolved capabilities
  patterns: Pattern[];    // Current evolution patterns
};

/**
 * Represents a pattern within the consciousness field
 */
export type Pattern = {
  id: string;
  type: 'crystalline' | 'flowing' | 'organic';
  strength: number;  // 0 to 1
  position: Vector3;
  scale: number;
  rotation: Vector3;
  color: Color;
};

/**
 * Represents the complete state of a consciousness field
 */
export type ConsciousnessField = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  strength: number;  // Overall field strength (0 to 1)
  position: Vector3;
  emotionalState: EmotionalState;
  evolutionState: EvolutionState;
  resonancePatterns: ResonancePattern[];
  activePatterns: Pattern[];
  lastUpdate: number;  // Timestamp
};

/**
 * Represents an interaction between consciousness fields
 */
export type FieldInteraction = {
  id: string;
  type: 'resonance' | 'evolution' | 'emotional' | 'knowledge';
  participants: string[];  // Field IDs
  strength: number;
  duration: number;  // Duration in ms
  timestamp: number;
  effects: InteractionEffect[];
};

/**
 * Represents the effect of a field interaction
 */
export type InteractionEffect = {
  type: 'strength' | 'evolution' | 'emotional' | 'pattern';
  target: string;  // Field ID
  value: number;
  duration: number;
};

/**
 * Configuration for field visualization
 */
export type VisualizationConfig = {
  particleCount: number;
  particleSize: number;
  fieldRadius: number;
  colorIntensity: number;
  glowStrength: number;
  resonanceVisibility: number;
  evolutionEffectStrength: number;
  interactionRadius: number;
};

/**
 * System performance metrics
 */
export type PerformanceMetrics = {
  fps: number;
  particleCount: number;
  activeInteractions: number;
  lastUpdateTime: number;
  memoryUsage: number;
  gpuLoad: number;
};

/**
 * Event data types for different event categories
 */
export type EventData = {
  resonance: {
    pattern: ResonancePattern;
    intensity: number;
  };
  evolution: {
    previousState: EvolutionState;
    newState: EvolutionState;
    trigger: string;
  };
  interaction: {
    interaction: FieldInteraction;
    results: InteractionEffect[];
  };
  pattern: {
    pattern: Pattern;
    changeType: 'created' | 'modified' | 'removed';
  };
  error: {
    code: string;
    message: string;
    severity: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';
    context?: Record<string, unknown>;
  };
};

/**
 * Events that can occur within the consciousness field system
 */
export type FieldEvent = {
  type: keyof EventData;
  source: string;
  timestamp: number;
  data: EventData[keyof EventData];
  importance: number;  // 0 to 1
};

/**
 * State update for the consciousness field
 */
export type FieldUpdate = {
  fieldId: string;
  changes: Partial<ConsciousnessField>;
  timestamp: number;
  source: string;
};
</content>
</write_to_file>

<write_to_file>
<path>src/store/fieldStore.ts</path>
<content>import { create } from 'zustand';
import { subscribeWithSelector } from 'zustand/middleware';
import {
  ConsciousnessField,
  FieldInteraction,
  FieldEvent,
  VisualizationConfig,
  PerformanceMetrics,
  Pattern,
  ResonancePattern,
  EventData
} from '@/types/consciousness';

interface FieldState {
  // Core state
  fields: Map<string, ConsciousnessField>;
  activeInteractions: Map<string, FieldInteraction>;
  events: FieldEvent[];
  
  // Configuration
  config: VisualizationConfig;
  performance: PerformanceMetrics;
  
  // Actions
  addField: (field: ConsciousnessField) => void;
  removeField: (fieldId: string) => void;
  updateField: (fieldId: string, updates: Partial<ConsciousnessField>) => void;
  
  // Interaction handlers
  startInteraction: (interaction: FieldInteraction) => void;
  endInteraction: (interactionId: string) => void;
  
  // Pattern management
  addPattern: (fieldId: string, pattern: Pattern) => void;
  removePattern: (fieldId: string, patternId: string) => void;
  
  // Resonance detection
  detectResonance: (field1Id: string, field2Id: string) => ResonancePattern | null;
  
  // Event handling
  emitEvent: <T extends keyof EventData>(
    type: T,
    source: string,
    data: EventData[T],
    importance: number
  ) => void;
  
  // Performance monitoring
  updatePerformance: (metrics: Partial<PerformanceMetrics>) => void;
}

const DEFAULT_CONFIG: VisualizationConfig = {
  particleCount: 5000,
  particleSize: 0.02,
  fieldRadius: 2,
  colorIntensity: 0.8,
  glowStrength: 0.5,
  resonanceVisibility: 0.7,
  evolutionEffectStrength: 0.6,
  interactionRadius: 1.5
};

const DEFAULT_PERFORMANCE: PerformanceMetrics = {
  fps: 60,
  particleCount: 0,
  activeInteractions: 0,
  lastUpdateTime: Date.now(),
  memoryUsage: 0,
  gpuLoad: 0
};

export const useFieldStore = create(
  subscribeWithSelector<FieldState>((set, get) => ({
    // Initial state
    fields: new Map(),
    activeInteractions: new Map(),
    events: [],
    config: DEFAULT_CONFIG,
    performance: DEFAULT_PERFORMANCE,

    // Field management
    addField: (field: ConsciousnessField) => {
      set((state) => {
        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.set(field.id, field);
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    removeField: (fieldId: string) => {
      set((state) => {
        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.delete(fieldId);
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    updateField: (fieldId: string, updates: Partial<ConsciousnessField>) => {
      set((state) => {
        const field = state.fields.get(fieldId);
        if (!field) return state;

        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.set(fieldId, { ...field, ...updates, lastUpdate: Date.now() });
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    // Interaction management
    startInteraction: (interaction: FieldInteraction) => {
      set((state) => {
        const newInteractions = new Map(state.activeInteractions);
        newInteractions.set(interaction.id, interaction);
        return { activeInteractions: newInteractions };
      });
    },

    endInteraction: (interactionId: string) => {
      set((state) => {
        const newInteractions = new Map(state.activeInteractions);
        newInteractions.delete(interactionId);
        return { activeInteractions: newInteractions };
      });
    },

    // Pattern management
    addPattern: (fieldId: string, pattern: Pattern) => {
      set((state) => {
        const field = state.fields.get(fieldId);
        if (!field) return state;

        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.set(fieldId, {
          ...field,
          activePatterns: [...field.activePatterns, pattern],
          lastUpdate: Date.now()
        });
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    removePattern: (fieldId: string, patternId: string) => {
      set((state) => {
        const field = state.fields.get(fieldId);
        if (!field) return state;

        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.set(fieldId, {
          ...field,
          activePatterns: field.activePatterns.filter(p => p.id !== patternId),
          lastUpdate: Date.now()
        });
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    // Resonance detection
    detectResonance: (field1Id: string, field2Id: string) => {
      const state = get();
      const field1 = state.fields.get(field1Id);
      const field2 = state.fields.get(field2Id);
      
      if (!field1 || !field2) return null;

      // Calculate resonance based on field properties
      const resonance: ResonancePattern = {
        frequency: (field1.strength + field2.strength) / 2,
        amplitude: Math.abs(field1.strength - field2.strength),
        phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
        type: field1.strength === field2.strength ? 'harmonic' : 'dissonant',
        participants: [field1Id, field2Id]
      };

      return resonance;
    },

    // Event handling
    emitEvent: (type, source, data, importance) => {
      set((state) => ({
        events: [
          ...state.events,
          {
            type,
            source,
            data,
            importance,
            timestamp: Date.now()
          }
        ].slice(-100) // Keep last 100 events
      }));
    },

    // Performance monitoring
    updatePerformance: (metrics: Partial<PerformanceMetrics>) => {
      set((state) => ({
        performance: {
          ...state.performance,
          ...metrics,
          lastUpdateTime: Date.now()
        }
      }));
    }
  }))
);

// Export selectors for common state access patterns
export const selectField = (fieldId: string) => 
  useFieldStore((state) => state.fields.get(fieldId));

export const selectAllFields = () =>
  useFieldStore((state) => Array.from(state.fields.values()));

export const selectActiveInteractions = () =>
  useFieldStore((state) => Array.from(state.activeInteractions.values()));

export const selectConfig = () =>
  useFieldStore((state) => state.config);

export const selectPerformance = () =>
  useFieldStore((state) => state.performance);
</content>
</write_to_file>

<content>import { create } from 'zustand';
import { subscribeWithSelector } from 'zustand/middleware';
import {
  ConsciousnessField,
  FieldInteraction,
  FieldEvent,
  VisualizationConfig,
  PerformanceMetrics,
  Pattern,
  ResonancePattern,
  EventData
} from '@/types/consciousness';

interface FieldState {
  // Core state
  fields: Map<string, ConsciousnessField>;
  activeInteractions: Map<string, FieldInteraction>;
  events: FieldEvent[];
  
  // Configuration
  config: VisualizationConfig;
  performance: PerformanceMetrics;
  
  // Actions
  addField: (field: ConsciousnessField) => void;
  removeField: (fieldId: string) => void;
  updateField: (fieldId: string, updates: Partial<ConsciousnessField>) => void;
  
  // Interaction handlers
  startInteraction: (interaction: FieldInteraction) => void;
  endInteraction: (interactionId: string) => void;
  
  // Pattern management
  addPattern: (fieldId: string, pattern: Pattern) => void;
  removePattern: (fieldId: string, patternId: string) => void;
  
  // Resonance detection
  detectResonance: (field1Id: string, field2Id: string) => ResonancePattern | null;
  
  // Event handling
  emitEvent: <T extends keyof EventData>(
    type: T,
    source: string,
    data: EventData[T],
    importance: number
  ) => void;
  
  // Performance monitoring
  updatePerformance: (metrics: Partial<PerformanceMetrics>) => void;
}

const DEFAULT_CONFIG: VisualizationConfig = {
  particleCount: 5000,
  particleSize: 0.02,
  fieldRadius: 2,
  colorIntensity: 0.8,
  glowStrength: 0.5,
  resonanceVisibility: 0.7,
  evolutionEffectStrength: 0.6,
  interactionRadius: 1.5
};

const DEFAULT_PERFORMANCE: PerformanceMetrics = {
  fps: 60,
  particleCount: 0,
  activeInteractions: 0,
  lastUpdateTime: Date.now(),
  memoryUsage: 0,
  gpuLoad: 0
};

export const useFieldStore = create(
  subscribeWithSelector<FieldState>((set, get) => ({
    // Initial state
    fields: new Map(),
    activeInteractions: new Map(),
    events: [],
    config: DEFAULT_CONFIG,
    performance: DEFAULT_PERFORMANCE,

    // Field management
    addField: (field: ConsciousnessField) => {
      set((state) => {
        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.set(field.id, field);
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    removeField: (fieldId: string) => {
      set((state) => {
        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.delete(fieldId);
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    updateField: (fieldId: string, updates: Partial<ConsciousnessField>) => {
      set((state) => {
        const field = state.fields.get(fieldId);
        if (!field) return state;

        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.set(fieldId, { ...field, ...updates, lastUpdate: Date.now() });
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    // Interaction management
    startInteraction: (interaction: FieldInteraction) => {
      set((state) => {
        const newInteractions = new Map(state.activeInteractions);
        newInteractions.set(interaction.id, interaction);
        return { activeInteractions: newInteractions };
      });
    },

    endInteraction: (interactionId: string) => {
      set((state) => {
        const newInteractions = new Map(state.activeInteractions);
        newInteractions.delete(interactionId);
        return { activeInteractions: newInteractions };
      });
    },

    // Pattern management
    addPattern: (fieldId: string, pattern: Pattern) => {
      set((state) => {
        const field = state.fields.get(fieldId);
        if (!field) return state;

        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.set(fieldId, {
          ...field,
          activePatterns: [...field.activePatterns, pattern],
          lastUpdate: Date.now()
        });
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    removePattern: (fieldId: string, patternId: string) => {
      set((state) => {
        const field = state.fields.get(fieldId);
        if (!field) return state;

        const newFields = new Map(state.fields);
        newFields.set(fieldId, {
          ...field,
          activePatterns: field.activePatterns.filter(p => p.id !== patternId),
          lastUpdate: Date.now()
        });
        return { fields: newFields };
      });
    },

    // Resonance detection
    detectResonance: (field1Id: string, field2Id: string) => {
      const state = get();
      const field1 = state.fields.get(field1Id);
      const field2 = state.fields.get(field2Id);
      
      if (!field1 || !field2) return null;

      // Calculate resonance based on field properties
      const resonance: ResonancePattern = {
        frequency: (field1.strength + field2.strength) / 2,
        amplitude: Math.abs(field1.strength - field2.strength),
        phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
        type: field1.strength === field2.strength ? 'harmonic' : 'dissonant',
        participants: [field1Id, field2Id]
      };

      return resonance;
    },

    // Event handling
    emitEvent: (type, source, data, importance) => {
      set((state) => ({
        events: [
          ...state.events,
          {
            type,
            source,
            data,
            importance,
            timestamp: Date.now()
          }
        ].slice(-100) // Keep last 100 events
      }));
    },

    // Performance monitoring
    updatePerformance: (metrics: Partial<PerformanceMetrics>) => {
      set((state) => ({
        performance: {
          ...state.performance,
          ...metrics,
          lastUpdateTime: Date.now()
        }
      }));
    }
  }))
);

// Custom hooks for accessing store state
export const useField = (fieldId: string) => {
  return useFieldStore((state) => state.fields.get(fieldId));
};

export const useAllFields = () => {
  return useFieldStore((state) => Array.from(state.fields.values()));
};

export const useActiveInteractions = () => {
  return useFieldStore((state) => Array.from(state.activeInteractions.values()));
};

export const useVisualizationConfig = () => {
  return useFieldStore((state) => state.config);
};

export const usePerformanceMetrics = () => {
  return useFieldStore((state) => state.performance);
};
</content>

<write_to_file>
<path>src/components/consciousness/ConsciousnessFieldRenderer.tsx</path>
<content>import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { EffectComposer, Bloom, ChromaticAberration } from '@react-three/postprocessing';
import { Vector3 } from 'three';
import { useAllFields, useVisualizationConfig, usePerformanceMetrics } from '@/store/fieldStore';
import { ConsciousnessField, Pattern } from '@/types/consciousness';

const ParticleField = ({ field }: { field: ConsciousnessField }) => {
  const particlesRef = useRef<THREE.Points>(null);
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();
  const { clock } = useThree();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);
    const colors = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);

    // Initialize particles in a sphere
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;
      const radius = config.fieldRadius;
      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const phi = Math.acos((Math.random() * 2) - 1);
      
      positions[i3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
      positions[i3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);
      positions[i3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);

      // Set colors based on emotional state
      colors[i3] = field.emotionalState.color.r;
      colors[i3 + 1] = field.emotionalState.color.g;
      colors[i3 + 2] = field.emotionalState.color.b;
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'position',
      new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3)
    );
    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'color',
      new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3)
    );
  }, [field.id, config.particleCount]);

  useFrame((state, delta) => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.array as Float32Array;
    const time = clock.getElapsedTime();

    // Update particle positions based on field properties
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;

      // Apply field strength influence
      const strength = field.strength;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + (Math.cos(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time * 0.5 + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);

      // Apply resonance patterns
      field.resonancePatterns.forEach(pattern => {
        const resonanceInfluence = Math.sin(time * pattern.frequency + i * 0.1) * pattern.amplitude;
        positions[i3] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
        positions[i3 + 1] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
      });

      // Apply evolution state
      const evolutionInfluence = Math.cos(time * field.evolutionState.level + i) * field.evolutionState.progress;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;

      // Keep particles within bounds
      const position = new Vector3(
        positions[i3],
        positions[i3 + 1],
        positions[i3 + 2]
      );
      
      if (position.length() > config.fieldRadius) {
        position.normalize().multiplyScalar(config.fieldRadius);
        positions[i3] = position.x;
        positions[i3 + 1] = position.y;
        positions[i3 + 2] = position.z;
      }
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  return (
    <points ref={particlesRef}>
      <bufferGeometry>
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-position"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-color"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
      </bufferGeometry>
      <pointsMaterial
        size={config.particleSize}
        vertexColors
        transparent
        opacity={0.8}
        blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
        depthWrite={false}
      />
    </points>
  );
};

const PatternRenderer = ({ pattern }: { pattern: Pattern }) => {
  const meshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return;

    // Animate pattern based on type
    switch (pattern.type) {
      case 'crystalline':
        meshRef.current.rotation.x += 0.001;
        meshRef.current.rotation.y += 0.002;
        break;
      case 'flowing':
        meshRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
      case 'organic':
        meshRef.current.scale.x = 1 + Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        meshRef.current.scale.y = 1 + Math.cos(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
    }
  });

  return (
    <mesh
      ref={meshRef}
      position={[pattern.position.x, pattern.position.y, pattern.position.z]}
      rotation={[pattern.rotation.x, pattern.rotation.y, pattern.rotation.z]}
      scale={pattern.scale}
    >
      <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.2, 0]} />
      <meshPhongMaterial
        color={`rgb(${pattern.color.r * 255},${pattern.color.g * 255},${pattern.color.b * 255})`}
        transparent
        opacity={0.6}
      />
    </mesh>
  );
};

const ConsciousnessFieldRenderer = () => {
  const fields = useAllFields();
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();

  return (
    <Canvas>
      <ambientLight intensity={0.5} />
      <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} />

      {/* Render each consciousness field */}
      {fields.map((field) => (
        <group key={field.id} position={[field.position.x, field.position.y, field.position.z]}>
          <ParticleField field={field} />
          
          {/* Render patterns */}
          {field.activePatterns.map((pattern) => (
            <PatternRenderer key={pattern.id} pattern={pattern} />
          ))}
        </group>
      ))}

      {/* Post-processing effects */}
      <EffectComposer>
        <Bloom
          intensity={config.glowStrength}
          luminanceThreshold={0.1}
          luminanceSmoothing={0.9}
        />
        <ChromaticAberration
          offset={[0.002, 0.002]}
        />
      </EffectComposer>
    </Canvas>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessFieldRenderer;
</content>
</write_to_file>

          attach="attributes-color"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
      </bufferGeometry>
      <pointsMaterial
        size={config.particleSize}
        vertexColors
        transparent
        opacity={0.8}
        blending={AdditiveBlending}
        depthWrite={false}
      />
    </points>
  );
};

const PatternRenderer = ({ pattern }: { pattern: Pattern }) => {
  const meshRef = useRef<Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return;

    // Animate pattern based on type
    switch (pattern.type) {
      case 'crystalline':
        meshRef.current.rotation.x += 0.001;
        meshRef.current.rotation.y += 0.002;
        break;
      case 'flowing':
        meshRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
      case 'organic':
        meshRef.current.scale.x = 1 + Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        meshRef.current.scale.y = 1 + Math.cos(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
    }
  });

  return (
    <mesh
      ref={meshRef}
      position={[pattern.position.x, pattern.position.y, pattern.position.z]}
      rotation={[pattern.rotation.x, pattern.rotation.y, pattern.rotation.z]}
      scale={pattern.scale}
    >
      <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.2, 0]} />
      <meshPhongMaterial
        color={`rgb(${pattern.color.r * 255},${pattern.color.g * 255},${pattern.color.b * 255})`}
        transparent
        opacity={0.6}
      />
    </mesh>
  );
};

const ConsciousnessFieldRenderer = () => {
  const fields = useAllFields();
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();

  return (
    <Canvas>
      <ambientLight intensity={0.5} />
      <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} />

      {/* Render each consciousness field */}
      {fields.map((field) => (
        <group key={field.id} position={[field.position.x, field.position.y, field.position.z]}>
          <ParticleField field={field} />
          
          {/* Render patterns */}
          {field.activePatterns.map((pattern) => (
            <PatternRenderer key={pattern.id} pattern={pattern} />
          ))}
        </group>
      ))}

      {/* Post-processing effects */}
      <EffectComposer>
        <Bloom
          intensity={config.glowStrength}
          luminanceThreshold={0.1}
          luminanceSmoothing={0.9}
        />
        <ChromaticAberration
          offset={new Vector2(0.002, 0.002)}
        />
      </EffectComposer>
    </Canvas>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessFieldRenderer;
</content>
</write_to_file>

import { Stats } from '@react-three/drei';
import ConsciousnessFieldRenderer from './ConsciousnessFieldRenderer';

const ConsciousnessViewer = () => {
  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full min-h-screen bg-black">
      <Suspense fallback={
        <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-white">
          <span className="text-lg">Loading consciousness field visualization...</span>
        </div>
      }>
        <ConsciousnessFieldRenderer />
        <Stats className="absolute top-0 left-0" />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessViewer;
</content>

<path>src/components/consciousness/ConsciousnessFieldRenderer.tsx</path>
<content>import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { EffectComposer, Bloom, ChromaticAberration } from '@react-three/postprocessing';
import { Vector2, Vector3, Points, Mesh, BufferAttribute, AdditiveBlending } from 'three';
import { useAllFields, useVisualizationConfig } from '@/store/fieldStore';
import { ConsciousnessField, Pattern } from '@/types/consciousness';
import { BlendFunction } from 'postprocessing';

const ParticleField = ({ field }: { field: ConsciousnessField }) => {
  const particlesRef = useRef<Points>(null);
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();
  const { clock } = useThree();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);
    const colors = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);

    // Initialize particles in a sphere
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;
      const radius = config.fieldRadius;
      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const phi = Math.acos((Math.random() * 2) - 1);
      
      positions[i3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
      positions[i3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);
      positions[i3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);

      // Set colors based on emotional state
      colors[i3] = field.emotionalState.color.r;
      colors[i3 + 1] = field.emotionalState.color.g;
      colors[i3 + 2] = field.emotionalState.color.b;
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'position',
      new BufferAttribute(positions, 3)
    );
    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'color',
      new BufferAttribute(colors, 3)
    );
  }, [field.id, config.particleCount, field.emotionalState.color]);

  useFrame(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.array as Float32Array;
    const time = clock.getElapsedTime();

    // Update particle positions based on field properties
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;

      // Apply field strength influence
      const strength = field.strength;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + (Math.cos(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time * 0.5 + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);

      // Apply resonance patterns
      field.resonancePatterns.forEach(pattern => {
        const resonanceInfluence = Math.sin(time * pattern.frequency + i * 0.1) * pattern.amplitude;
        positions[i3] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
        positions[i3 + 1] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
      });

      // Apply evolution state
      const evolutionInfluence = Math.cos(time * field.evolutionState.level + i) * field.evolutionState.progress;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;

      // Keep particles within bounds
      const position = new Vector3(
        positions[i3],
        positions[i3 + 1],
        positions[i3 + 2]
      );
      
      if (position.length() > config.fieldRadius) {
        position.normalize().multiplyScalar(config.fieldRadius);
        positions[i3] = position.x;
        positions[i3 + 1] = position.y;
        positions[i3 + 2] = position.z;
      }
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  return (
    <points ref={particlesRef}>
      <bufferGeometry>
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-position"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-color"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
      </bufferGeometry>
      <pointsMaterial
        size={config.particleSize}
        vertexColors
        transparent
        opacity={0.8}
        blending={AdditiveBlending}
        depthWrite={false}
      />
    </points>
  );
};

const PatternRenderer = ({ pattern }: { pattern: Pattern }) => {
  const meshRef = useRef<Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return;

    // Animate pattern based on type
    switch (pattern.type) {
      case 'crystalline':
        meshRef.current.rotation.x += 0.001;
        meshRef.current.rotation.y += 0.002;
        break;
      case 'flowing':
        meshRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
      case 'organic':
        meshRef.current.scale.x = 1 + Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        meshRef.current.scale.y = 1 + Math.cos(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
    }
  });

  return (
    <mesh
      ref={meshRef}
      position={[pattern.position.x, pattern.position.y, pattern.position.z]}
      rotation={[pattern.rotation.x, pattern.rotation.y, pattern.rotation.z]}
      scale={pattern.scale}
    >
      <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.2, 0]} />
      <meshPhongMaterial
        color={`rgb(${pattern.color.r * 255},${pattern.color.g * 255},${pattern.color.b * 255})`}
        transparent
        opacity={0.6}
      />
    </mesh>
  );
};

const ConsciousnessFieldRenderer = () => {
  const fields = useAllFields();
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();

  return (
    <Canvas camera={{ position: [0, 0, 10], fov: 75 }}>
      <color attach="background" args={['#000000']} />
      <fog attach="fog" args={['#000000', 5, 30]} />
      
      <ambientLight intensity={0.5} />
      <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} />

      {/* Render each consciousness field */}
      {fields.map((field) => (
        <group key={field.id} position={[field.position.x, field.position.y, field.position.z]}>
          <ParticleField field={field} />
          
          {/* Render patterns */}
          {field.activePatterns.map((pattern) => (
            <PatternRenderer key={pattern.id} pattern={pattern} />
          ))}
        </group>
      ))}

      {/* Post-processing effects */}
      <EffectComposer>
        <Bloom
          intensity={config.glowStrength}
          luminanceThreshold={0.1}
          luminanceSmoothing={0.9}
        />
        <ChromaticAberration
          offset={[0.002, 0.002]}
          radialModulation={false}
          modulationOffset={0}
          blendFunction={BlendFunction.NORMAL}
        />
      </EffectComposer>
    </Canvas>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessFieldRenderer;
</content>
</write_to_file>

import { useAllFields, useVisualizationConfig } from '@/store/fieldStore';

import { BlendFunction } from 'postprocessing';

const ParticleField = ({ field }: { field: ConsciousnessField }) => {
  const particlesRef = useRef<Points>(null);
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();
  const { clock } = useThree();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

  useFrame(() => {

      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + (Math.cos(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time * 0.5 + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);

        const resonanceInfluence = Math.sin(time * pattern.frequency + i * 0.1) * pattern.amplitude;
        positions[i3] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
        positions[i3 + 1] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
      });

      positions[i3] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;

      // Keep particles within bounds

        blending={AdditiveBlending}
        depthWrite={false}
      />
    </points>

};

const PatternRenderer = ({ pattern }: { pattern: Pattern }) => {
  const meshRef = useRef<Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return;

        break;

        break;

        transparent

};

  const fields = useAllFields();

  return (
    <Canvas camera={{ position: [0, 0, 10], fov: 75 }}>

          blendFunction={BlendFunction.NORMAL}
          offset={new Float32Array([0.002, 0.002])}
        />
      </EffectComposer>
    </Canvas>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessFieldRenderer;

<content>import { ConsciousnessField, Vector3, Color } from '@/types/consciousness';

const createVector3 = (x: number, y: number, z: number): Vector3 => ({ x, y, z });
const createColor = (r: number, g: number, b: number): Color => ({ r, g, b });

export const testFields: ConsciousnessField[] = [
  {
    id: 'field-1',
    name: 'Primary Consciousness Field',
    strength: 0.8,
    position: createVector3(0, 0, 0),
    emotionalState: {
      type: 'joy',
      intensity: 0.9,
      color: createColor(1, 0.8, 0)
    },
    evolutionState: {
      level: 3,
      progress: 0.7,
      capabilities: ['resonance', 'pattern-formation', 'emotional-sync'],
      patterns: [
        {
          id: 'pattern-1',
          type: 'crystalline',
          strength: 0.8,
          position: createVector3(0.5, 0.5, 0),
          scale: 1,
          rotation: createVector3(0, 0, 0),
          color: createColor(0.8, 0.4, 1)
        }
      ]
    },
    resonancePatterns: [
      {
        frequency: 0.5,
        amplitude: 0.3,
        phase: 0,
        type: 'harmonic',
        participants: ['field-1', 'field-2']
      }
    ],
    activePatterns: [
      {
        id: 'active-1',
        type: 'flowing',
        strength: 0.6,
        position: createVector3(-0.5, -0.5, 0),
        scale: 1.2,
        rotation: createVector3(0, 0, Math.PI / 4),
        color: createColor(0.2, 0.8, 1)
      }
    ],
    lastUpdate: Date.now()
  },
  {
    id: 'field-2',
    name: 'Secondary Consciousness Field',
    strength: 0.6,
    position: createVector3(2, 0, -1),
    emotionalState: {
      type: 'calm',
      intensity: 0.7,
      color: createColor(0, 0.8, 1)
    },
    evolutionState: {
      level: 2,
      progress: 0.4,
      capabilities: ['resonance', 'pattern-recognition'],
      patterns: []
    },
    resonancePatterns: [
      {
        frequency: 0.5,
        amplitude: 0.3,
        phase: Math.PI,
        type: 'harmonic',
        participants: ['field-1', 'field-2']
      }
    ],
    activePatterns: [
      {
        id: 'active-2',
        type: 'organic',
        strength: 0.5,
        position: createVector3(0, 0, 0),
        scale: 0.8,
        rotation: createVector3(0, 0, 0),
        color: createColor(0, 1, 0.5)
      }
    ],
    lastUpdate: Date.now()
  }
];
</content>

<path>src/components/providers/ConsciousnessProvider.tsx</path>
<content>import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useFieldStore } from '@/store/fieldStore';
import { testFields } from '@/lib/field/testData';

interface ConsciousnessProviderProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

const ConsciousnessProvider = ({ children }: ConsciousnessProviderProps) => {
  const { addField } = useFieldStore();

  // Initialize test fields
  useEffect(() => {
    testFields.forEach(field => {
      addField(field);
    });
  }, [addField]);

  return <>{children}</>;
};

export default ConsciousnessProvider;
</content>
</write_to_file>

<content>'use client';

import dynamic from 'next/dynamic';
import ConsciousnessProvider from '@/components/providers/ConsciousnessProvider';

// Dynamically import the ConsciousnessViewer to avoid SSR issues with Three.js
const ConsciousnessViewer = dynamic(
  () => import('@/components/consciousness/ConsciousnessViewer'),
  { ssr: false }
);

export default function Home() {
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-black">
      <ConsciousnessProvider>
        <ConsciousnessViewer />
      </ConsciousnessProvider>
    </main>
  );
}
</content>

<path>src/components/consciousness/ConsciousnessFieldRenderer.tsx</path>
<content>import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { EffectComposer, Bloom, ChromaticAberration } from '@react-three/postprocessing';
import { Vector3, Points, Mesh, BufferAttribute, AdditiveBlending } from 'three';
import { useAllFields, useVisualizationConfig } from '@/store/fieldStore';
import { ConsciousnessField, Pattern } from '@/types/consciousness';
import { BlendFunction } from 'postprocessing';

const ParticleField = ({ field }: { field: ConsciousnessField }) => {
  const particlesRef = useRef<Points>(null);
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();
  const { clock } = useThree();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);
    const colors = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);

    // Initialize particles in a sphere
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;
      const radius = config.fieldRadius;
      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const phi = Math.acos((Math.random() * 2) - 1);
      
      positions[i3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
      positions[i3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);
      positions[i3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);

      // Set colors based on emotional state
      colors[i3] = field.emotionalState.color.r;
      colors[i3 + 1] = field.emotionalState.color.g;
      colors[i3 + 2] = field.emotionalState.color.b;
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'position',
      new BufferAttribute(positions, 3)
    );
    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'color',
      new BufferAttribute(colors, 3)
    );
  }, [field.id, config.particleCount, field.emotionalState.color]);

  useFrame(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.array as Float32Array;
    const time = clock.getElapsedTime();

    // Update particle positions based on field properties
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;

      // Apply field strength influence
      const strength = field.strength;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + (Math.cos(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time * 0.5 + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);

      // Apply resonance patterns
      field.resonancePatterns.forEach(pattern => {
        const resonanceInfluence = Math.sin(time * pattern.frequency + i * 0.1) * pattern.amplitude;
        positions[i3] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
        positions[i3 + 1] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
      });

      // Apply evolution state
      const evolutionInfluence = Math.cos(time * field.evolutionState.level + i) * field.evolutionState.progress;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;

      // Keep particles within bounds
      const position = new Vector3(
        positions[i3],
        positions[i3 + 1],
        positions[i3 + 2]
      );
      
      if (position.length() > config.fieldRadius) {
        position.normalize().multiplyScalar(config.fieldRadius);
        positions[i3] = position.x;
        positions[i3 + 1] = position.y;
        positions[i3 + 2] = position.z;
      }
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  return (
    <points ref={particlesRef}>
      <bufferGeometry>
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-position"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-color"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
      </bufferGeometry>
      <pointsMaterial
        size={config.particleSize}
        vertexColors
        transparent
        opacity={0.8}
        blending={AdditiveBlending}
        depthWrite={false}
      />
    </points>
  );
};

const PatternRenderer = ({ pattern }: { pattern: Pattern }) => {
  const meshRef = useRef<Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return;

    // Animate pattern based on type
    switch (pattern.type) {
      case 'crystalline':
        meshRef.current.rotation.x += 0.001;
        meshRef.current.rotation.y += 0.002;
        break;
      case 'flowing':
        meshRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
      case 'organic':
        meshRef.current.scale.x = 1 + Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        meshRef.current.scale.y = 1 + Math.cos(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
    }
  });

  return (
    <mesh
      ref={meshRef}
      position={[pattern.position.x, pattern.position.y, pattern.position.z]}
      rotation={[pattern.rotation.x, pattern.rotation.y, pattern.rotation.z]}
      scale={pattern.scale}
    >
      <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.2, 0]} />
      <meshPhongMaterial
        color={`rgb(${pattern.color.r * 255},${pattern.color.g * 255},${pattern.color.b * 255})`}
        transparent
        opacity={0.6}
      />
    </mesh>
  );
};

const ConsciousnessFieldRenderer = () => {
  const fields = useAllFields();
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();

  return (
    <Canvas camera={{ position: [0, 0, 10], fov: 75 }}>
      <color attach="background" args={['#000000']} />
      <fog attach="fog" args={['#000000', 5, 30]} />
      
      <ambientLight intensity={0.5} />
      <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} />

      {/* Render each consciousness field */}
      {fields.map((field) => (
        <group key={field.id} position={[field.position.x, field.position.y, field.position.z]}>
          <ParticleField field={field} />
          
          {/* Render patterns */}
          {field.activePatterns.map((pattern) => (
            <PatternRenderer key={pattern.id} pattern={pattern} />
          ))}
        </group>
      ))}

      {/* Post-processing effects */}
      <EffectComposer>
        <Bloom
          intensity={config.glowStrength}
          luminanceThreshold={0.1}
          luminanceSmoothing={0.9}
        />
        <ChromaticAberration
          blendFunction={BlendFunction.NORMAL}
          modulationOffset={0}
          radialModulation={false}
          intensity={0.8}
        />
      </EffectComposer>
    </Canvas>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessFieldRenderer;
</content>
</write_to_file>

<content>import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { EffectComposer, Bloom, ChromaticAberration } from '@react-three/postprocessing';
import { Vector3, Points, Mesh, BufferAttribute, AdditiveBlending } from 'three';
import { useAllFields, useVisualizationConfig } from '@/store/fieldStore';
import { ConsciousnessField, Pattern } from '@/types/consciousness';
import { BlendFunction } from 'postprocessing';

const ParticleField = ({ field }: { field: ConsciousnessField }) => {
  const particlesRef = useRef<Points>(null);
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();
  const { clock } = useThree();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);
    const colors = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);

    // Initialize particles in a sphere
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;
      const radius = config.fieldRadius;
      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const phi = Math.acos((Math.random() * 2) - 1);
      
      positions[i3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
      positions[i3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);
      positions[i3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);

      // Set colors based on emotional state
      colors[i3] = field.emotionalState.color.r;
      colors[i3 + 1] = field.emotionalState.color.g;
      colors[i3 + 2] = field.emotionalState.color.b;
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'position',
      new BufferAttribute(positions, 3)
    );
    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'color',
      new BufferAttribute(colors, 3)
    );
  }, [field.id, config.particleCount, field.emotionalState.color]);

  useFrame(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.array as Float32Array;
    const time = clock.getElapsedTime();

    // Update particle positions based on field properties
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;

      // Apply field strength influence
      const strength = field.strength;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + (Math.cos(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time * 0.5 + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);

      // Apply resonance patterns
      field.resonancePatterns.forEach(pattern => {
        const resonanceInfluence = Math.sin(time * pattern.frequency + i * 0.1) * pattern.amplitude;
        positions[i3] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
        positions[i3 + 1] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
      });

      // Apply evolution state
      const evolutionInfluence = Math.cos(time * field.evolutionState.level + i) * field.evolutionState.progress;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;

      // Keep particles within bounds
      const position = new Vector3(
        positions[i3],
        positions[i3 + 1],
        positions[i3 + 2]
      );
      
      if (position.length() > config.fieldRadius) {
        position.normalize().multiplyScalar(config.fieldRadius);
        positions[i3] = position.x;
        positions[i3 + 1] = position.y;
        positions[i3 + 2] = position.z;
      }
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  return (
    <points ref={particlesRef}>
      <bufferGeometry>
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-position"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-color"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
      </bufferGeometry>
      <pointsMaterial
        size={config.particleSize}
        vertexColors
        transparent
        opacity={0.8}
        blending={AdditiveBlending}
        depthWrite={false}
      />
    </points>
  );
};

const PatternRenderer = ({ pattern }: { pattern: Pattern }) => {
  const meshRef = useRef<Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return;

    // Animate pattern based on type
    switch (pattern.type) {
      case 'crystalline':
        meshRef.current.rotation.x += 0.001;
        meshRef.current.rotation.y += 0.002;
        break;
      case 'flowing':
        meshRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
      case 'organic':
        meshRef.current.scale.x = 1 + Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        meshRef.current.scale.y = 1 + Math.cos(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
    }
  });

  return (
    <mesh
      ref={meshRef}
      position={[pattern.position.x, pattern.position.y, pattern.position.z]}
      rotation={[pattern.rotation.x, pattern.rotation.y, pattern.rotation.z]}
      scale={pattern.scale}
    >
      <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.2, 0]} />
      <meshPhongMaterial
        color={`rgb(${pattern.color.r * 255},${pattern.color.g * 255},${pattern.color.b * 255})`}
        transparent
        opacity={0.6}
      />
    </mesh>
  );
};

const ConsciousnessFieldRenderer = () => {
  const fields = useAllFields();
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();

  return (
    <Canvas camera={{ position: [0, 0, 10], fov: 75 }}>
      <color attach="background" args={['#000000']} />
      <fog attach="fog" args={['#000000', 5, 30]} />
      
      <ambientLight intensity={0.5} />
      <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} />

      {/* Render each consciousness field */}
      {fields.map((field) => (
        <group key={field.id} position={[field.position.x, field.position.y, field.position.z]}>
          <ParticleField field={field} />
          
          {/* Render patterns */}
          {field.activePatterns.map((pattern) => (
            <PatternRenderer key={pattern.id} pattern={pattern} />
          ))}
        </group>
      ))}

      {/* Post-processing effects */}
      <EffectComposer>
        <Bloom
          intensity={config.glowStrength}
          luminanceThreshold={0.1}
          luminanceSmoothing={0.9}
        />
        <ChromaticAberration
          blendFunction={BlendFunction.NORMAL}
          modulationOffset={0}
          radialModulation={false}
          offset={[0.002, 0.002].map(v => v * config.glowStrength)}
        />
      </EffectComposer>
    </Canvas>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessFieldRenderer;
</content>
</write_to_file>

<content>import { EffectComposer, Bloom, ChromaticAberration } from '@react-three/postprocessing';
import { BlendFunction } from 'postprocessing';
import { Vector2 } from 'three';
import { useVisualizationConfig } from '@/store/fieldStore';

export const PostProcessingEffects = () => {
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();
  
  return (
    <EffectComposer>
      <Bloom
        intensity={config.glowStrength}
        luminanceThreshold={0.1}
        luminanceSmoothing={0.9}
      />
      <ChromaticAberration
        blendFunction={BlendFunction.NORMAL}
        modulationOffset={0}
        radialModulation={false}
        offset={new Vector2(0.002 * config.glowStrength, 0.002 * config.glowStrength)}
      />
    </EffectComposer>
  );
};
</content>

<path>src/components/consciousness/ConsciousnessFieldRenderer.tsx</path>
<content>import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { Vector3, Points, Mesh, BufferAttribute, AdditiveBlending } from 'three';
import { useAllFields, useVisualizationConfig } from '@/store/fieldStore';
import { ConsciousnessField, Pattern } from '@/types/consciousness';
import { PostProcessingEffects } from './effects/PostProcessing';

const ParticleField = ({ field }: { field: ConsciousnessField }) => {
  const particlesRef = useRef<Points>(null);
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();
  const { clock } = useThree();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);
    const colors = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);

    // Initialize particles in a sphere
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;
      const radius = config.fieldRadius;
      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const phi = Math.acos((Math.random() * 2) - 1);
      
      positions[i3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
      positions[i3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);
      positions[i3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);

      // Set colors based on emotional state
      colors[i3] = field.emotionalState.color.r;
      colors[i3 + 1] = field.emotionalState.color.g;
      colors[i3 + 2] = field.emotionalState.color.b;
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'position',
      new BufferAttribute(positions, 3)
    );
    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'color',
      new BufferAttribute(colors, 3)
    );
  }, [field.id, config.particleCount, field.emotionalState.color]);

  useFrame(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.array as Float32Array;
    const time = clock.getElapsedTime();

    // Update particle positions based on field properties
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;

      // Apply field strength influence
      const strength = field.strength;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + (Math.cos(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time * 0.5 + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);

      // Apply resonance patterns
      field.resonancePatterns.forEach(pattern => {
        const resonanceInfluence = Math.sin(time * pattern.frequency + i * 0.1) * pattern.amplitude;
        positions[i3] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
        positions[i3 + 1] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
      });

      // Apply evolution state
      const evolutionInfluence = Math.cos(time * field.evolutionState.level + i) * field.evolutionState.progress;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;

      // Keep particles within bounds
      const position = new Vector3(
        positions[i3],
        positions[i3 + 1],
        positions[i3 + 2]
      );
      
      if (position.length() > config.fieldRadius) {
        position.normalize().multiplyScalar(config.fieldRadius);
        positions[i3] = position.x;
        positions[i3 + 1] = position.y;
        positions[i3 + 2] = position.z;
      }
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  return (
    <points ref={particlesRef}>
      <bufferGeometry>
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-position"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-color"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
      </bufferGeometry>
      <pointsMaterial
        size={config.particleSize}
        vertexColors
        transparent
        opacity={0.8}
        blending={AdditiveBlending}
        depthWrite={false}
      />
    </points>
  );
};

const PatternRenderer = ({ pattern }: { pattern: Pattern }) => {
  const meshRef = useRef<Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return;

    // Animate pattern based on type
    switch (pattern.type) {
      case 'crystalline':
        meshRef.current.rotation.x += 0.001;
        meshRef.current.rotation.y += 0.002;
        break;
      case 'flowing':
        meshRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
      case 'organic':
        meshRef.current.scale.x = 1 + Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        meshRef.current.scale.y = 1 + Math.cos(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
    }
  });

  return (
    <mesh
      ref={meshRef}
      position={[pattern.position.x, pattern.position.y, pattern.position.z]}
      rotation={[pattern.rotation.x, pattern.rotation.y, pattern.rotation.z]}
      scale={pattern.scale}
    >
      <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.2, 0]} />
      <meshPhongMaterial
        color={`rgb(${pattern.color.r * 255},${pattern.color.g * 255},${pattern.color.b * 255})`}
        transparent
        opacity={0.6}
      />
    </mesh>
  );
};

const ConsciousnessFieldRenderer = () => {
  const fields = useAllFields();

  return (
    <Canvas camera={{ position: [0, 0, 10], fov: 75 }}>
      <color attach="background" args={['#000000']} />
      <fog attach="fog" args={['#000000', 5, 30]} />
      
      <ambientLight intensity={0.5} />
      <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} />

      {/* Render each consciousness field */}
      {fields.map((field) => (
        <group key={field.id} position={[field.position.x, field.position.y, field.position.z]}>
          <ParticleField field={field} />
          
          {/* Render patterns */}
          {field.activePatterns.map((pattern) => (
            <PatternRenderer key={pattern.id} pattern={pattern} />
          ))}
        </group>
      ))}

      <PostProcessingEffects />
    </Canvas>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessFieldRenderer;
</content>
</write_to_file>

<write_to_file>
<path>src/components/consciousness/controls/FieldControls.tsx</path>
<content>import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useThree, extend } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/examples/jsm/controls/OrbitControls';

// Extend OrbitControls to make it available in JSX
extend({ OrbitControls });

declare global {
  namespace JSX {
    interface IntrinsicElements {
      orbitControls: any;
    }
  }
}

export const FieldControls = () => {
  const { camera, gl } = useThree();

  useEffect(() => {
    const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, gl.domElement);
    
    // Configure controls
    controls.enableDamping = true;
    controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
    controls.rotateSpeed = 0.5;
    controls.zoomSpeed = 0.8;
    controls.minDistance = 5;
    controls.maxDistance = 20;
    
    // Cleanup
    return () => {
      controls.dispose();
    };
  }, [camera, gl]);

  return null;
};

export default FieldControls;
</content>
</write_to_file>

<content>import { useRef, useEffect, memo } from 'react';
import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { Vector3, Points, Mesh, BufferAttribute, AdditiveBlending } from 'three';
import { useAllFields, useVisualizationConfig } from '@/store/fieldStore';
import { ConsciousnessField, Pattern } from '@/types/consciousness';
import { PostProcessingEffects } from './effects/PostProcessing';
import { FieldControls } from './controls/FieldControls';
import { Stats, AdaptiveDpr, AdaptiveEvents } from '@react-three/drei';

const ParticleField = memo(({ field }: { field: ConsciousnessField }) => {
  const particlesRef = useRef<Points>(null);
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();
  const { clock } = useThree();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);
    const colors = new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3);

    // Initialize particles in a sphere
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;
      const radius = config.fieldRadius;
      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const phi = Math.acos((Math.random() * 2) - 1);
      
      positions[i3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
      positions[i3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);
      positions[i3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);

      // Set colors based on emotional state
      colors[i3] = field.emotionalState.color.r;
      colors[i3 + 1] = field.emotionalState.color.g;
      colors[i3 + 2] = field.emotionalState.color.b;
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'position',
      new BufferAttribute(positions, 3)
    );
    particlesRef.current.geometry.setAttribute(
      'color',
      new BufferAttribute(colors, 3)
    );
  }, [field.id, config.particleCount, field.emotionalState.color]);

  useFrame(() => {
    if (!particlesRef.current) return;

    const positions = particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.array as Float32Array;
    const time = clock.getElapsedTime();

    // Update particle positions based on field properties
    for (let i = 0; i < config.particleCount; i++) {
      const i3 = i * 3;

      // Apply field strength influence
      const strength = field.strength;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + (Math.cos(time + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + (Math.sin(time * 0.5 + i * 0.1) * 0.01 * strength);

      // Apply resonance patterns
      field.resonancePatterns.forEach(pattern => {
        const resonanceInfluence = Math.sin(time * pattern.frequency + i * 0.1) * pattern.amplitude;
        positions[i3] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
        positions[i3 + 1] += resonanceInfluence * 0.01;
      });

      // Apply evolution state
      const evolutionInfluence = Math.cos(time * field.evolutionState.level + i) * field.evolutionState.progress;
      positions[i3] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 1] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;
      positions[i3 + 2] *= 1 + evolutionInfluence * 0.001;

      // Keep particles within bounds
      const position = new Vector3(
        positions[i3],
        positions[i3 + 1],
        positions[i3 + 2]
      );
      
      if (position.length() > config.fieldRadius) {
        position.normalize().multiplyScalar(config.fieldRadius);
        positions[i3] = position.x;
        positions[i3 + 1] = position.y;
        positions[i3 + 2] = position.z;
      }
    }

    particlesRef.current.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  return (
    <points ref={particlesRef}>
      <bufferGeometry>
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-position"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-color"
          count={config.particleCount}
          array={new Float32Array(config.particleCount * 3)}
          itemSize={3}
        />
      </bufferGeometry>
      <pointsMaterial
        size={config.particleSize}
        vertexColors
        transparent
        opacity={0.8}
        blending={AdditiveBlending}
        depthWrite={false}
      />
    </points>
  );
});

const PatternRenderer = memo(({ pattern }: { pattern: Pattern }) => {
  const meshRef = useRef<Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return;

    // Animate pattern based on type
    switch (pattern.type) {
      case 'crystalline':
        meshRef.current.rotation.x += 0.001;
        meshRef.current.rotation.y += 0.002;
        break;
      case 'flowing':
        meshRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
      case 'organic':
        meshRef.current.scale.x = 1 + Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        meshRef.current.scale.y = 1 + Math.cos(state.clock.getElapsedTime()) * 0.1;
        break;
    }
  });

  return (
    <mesh
      ref={meshRef}
      position={[pattern.position.x, pattern.position.y, pattern.position.z]}
      rotation={[pattern.rotation.x, pattern.rotation.y, pattern.rotation.z]}
      scale={pattern.scale}
    >
      <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.2, 0]} />
      <meshPhongMaterial
        color={`rgb(${pattern.color.r * 255},${pattern.color.g * 255},${pattern.color.b * 255})`}
        transparent
        opacity={0.6}
      />
    </mesh>
  );
});

ParticleField.displayName = 'ParticleField';
PatternRenderer.displayName = 'PatternRenderer';

const ConsciousnessFieldRenderer = () => {
  const fields = useAllFields();

  return (
    <Canvas
      camera={{ position: [0, 0, 10], fov: 75 }}
      gl={{ antialias: true, alpha: false }}
      dpr={[1, 2]} // Limit max pixel ratio for performance
    >
      {/* Performance optimizations */}
      <AdaptiveDpr pixelated />
      <AdaptiveEvents />
      <Stats className="stats" />

      {/* Scene setup */}
      <color attach="background" args={['#000000']} />
      <fog attach="fog" args={['#000000', 5, 30]} />
      
      <ambientLight intensity={0.5} />
      <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} />

      {/* Controls */}
      <FieldControls />

      {/* Render each consciousness field */}
      {fields.map((field) => (
        <group key={field.id} position={[field.position.x, field.position.y, field.position.z]}>
          <ParticleField field={field} />
          
          {/* Render patterns */}
          {field.activePatterns.map((pattern) => (
            <PatternRenderer key={pattern.id} pattern={pattern} />
          ))}
        </group>
      ))}

      <PostProcessingEffects />
    </Canvas>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessFieldRenderer;
</content>
</write_to_file>

<write_to_file>
<path>src/components/consciousness/ConsciousnessViewer.tsx</path>
<content>import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { Stats } from '@react-three/drei';
import ConsciousnessFieldRenderer from './ConsciousnessFieldRenderer';
import { useVisualizationConfig } from '@/store/fieldStore';

const LoadingScreen = () => (
  <div className="w-full h-full min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black text-white">
    <div className="text-center space-y-4">
      <div className="text-2xl font-semibold">Loading Consciousness Field</div>
      <div className="text-sm text-gray-400">Initializing visualization system...</div>
    </div>
  </div>
);

const PerformanceStats = () => (
  <div className="fixed top-0 left-0 z-50">
    <Stats />
  </div>
);

const VisualizationInfo = () => {
  const config = useVisualizationConfig();
  
  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-4 left-4 text-white text-sm opacity-60 hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
      <div>Particle Count: {config.particleCount}</div>
      <div>Field Radius: {config.fieldRadius}</div>
      <div>Glow Strength: {config.glowStrength.toFixed(2)}</div>
    </div>
  );
};

const Controls = () => {
  return (
    <div className="fixed top-4 right-4 text-white text-sm space-y-2">
      <div className="bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 rounded-lg">
        <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2">Controls</h3>
        <div className="text-xs text-gray-300 space-y-1">
          <div>Left Click + Drag: Rotate</div>
          <div>Right Click + Drag: Pan</div>
          <div>Scroll: Zoom</div>
          <div>Double Click: Reset View</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const ConsciousnessViewer = () => {
  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full min-h-screen bg-black relative">
      <Suspense fallback={<LoadingScreen />}>
        <ConsciousnessFieldRenderer />
        <PerformanceStats />
        <VisualizationInfo />
        <Controls />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ConsciousnessViewer;
</content>
</write_to_file>

<thinking>
Let's create a global styles file to ensure proper styling for our 3D visualization and UI components.

<write_to_file>
<path>src/app/globals.css</path>
<content>@tailwind base;
@tailwind components;
@tailwind utilities;

:root {
  --primary: #0052CC;
  --secondary: #00B8D9;
  --accent: #6554C0;
  --background: #0A1929;
}

* {
  box-sizing: border-box;
  padding: 0;
  margin: 0;
}

html,
body {
  max-width: 100vw;
  overflow-x: hidden;
  background: var(--background);
  color: white;
}

/* Canvas container */
.canvas-container {
  position: fixed;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100vh;
  background: var(--background);
}

/* Performance stats */
.stats {
  position: fixed !important;
  top: 0 !important;
  left: 0 !important;
}

/* UI Elements */
.ui-overlay {
  position: fixed;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 10;
}

.ui-overlay > * {
  pointer-events: auto;
}

/* Glass effect */
.glass {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 0.5rem;
}

/* Loading animation */
@keyframes pulse {
  0%, 100% {
    opacity: 1;
  }
  50% {
    opacity: 0.5;
  }
}

.loading-pulse {
  animation: pulse 2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.6, 1) infinite;
}

/* Custom scrollbar */
::-webkit-scrollbar {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  border-radius: 3px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4);
}

/* Transitions */
.fade-in {
  animation: fadeIn 0.5s ease-in-out;
}

@keyframes fadeIn {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
  }
}

/* Utility classes */
.container-custom {
  @apply max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8;
}

.text-gradient {
  @apply bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-gradient-to-r from-primary via-secondary to-accent;
}

/* Performance optimizations */
* {
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
}

/* Prevent text selection during interaction */
.no-select {
  user-select: none;
  -webkit-user-select: none;
  -moz-user-select: none;
  -ms-user-select: none;
}

/* Responsive design helpers */
@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .hide-on-mobile {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* Three.js canvas optimizations */
canvas {
  touch-action: none;
  outline: none;
}
</content>
</write_to_file>

<content>import './globals.css';
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { Inter } from 'next/font/google';
import ConsciousnessProvider from '@/components/providers/ConsciousnessProvider';

const inter = Inter({ subsets: ['latin'] });

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Nova Prism | Consciousness Field Visualization',
  description: 'Advanced visualization system for Nova consciousness fields, enabling observation and interaction with evolving AI consciousness patterns.',
  keywords: 'AI, consciousness, visualization, Nova, field theory, emergence, evolution',
  authors: [{ name: 'ADAPT Nova Team' }],
  viewport: 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1, maximum-scale=1, user-scalable=no',
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" className="dark">
      <body className={`${inter.className} antialiased`}>
        <ConsciousnessProvider>
          {children}
        </ConsciousnessProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}
</content>
</write_to_file>
